- We fix sum S=n (scale). At each iteration:

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300):
  deadline = time.monotonic() + budget_s - 10
  get parent; n=parent.size
  def norm(a): a=a.clip(0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return ones; return a * n/s  # mean 1
  # fast conv via FFT
  def conv_fft(a, N): A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N); return C[:L]
  def fast_score(a): B=conv_fft(a,Nc); M=B.max(); return 2.0*n*M/n**2 = 2*M/n (since sum n). But for exact, use evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
  # initial candidates: parent norm, ones, sinusoids with freqs (1,2,3), phases; maybe low-pass random; normalized.
  # exact evaluate top few, track best; choose best for optimization.
  # Optimization function optimize(a0, time_alloc):
      a=norm(a0); M0=fast_score; best_a=a.copy(); best_fast=M0/n?
      iteration loop until time.
      schedule β/T or K over progress; use active weights.
      compute B every iter (needed for M and weights). gradient.
      additive line search:
         d = corr(w,a) (maybe scaled factor 2 omitted)
         d -= mean(d)
         # optionally normalize d by its max abs to set step range
         maxd = max(|d|)
         if maxd < tol: break
         # step start: want relative change ~0.1 at max. alpha = 0.1 * mean(a)/maxd? Since a~1.
         alpha = 0.1 * np.mean(a) / maxd # if d scaled.
         # upper bound for positivity: if d>0, alpha<=0.9*a/d; take min if finite
         pos = d>1e-15; if any: upper=0.9*np.min(a[pos]/d[pos]); alpha=min(alpha, upper)
         alpha = max(alpha, 1e-8)
         # if alpha too small, try multiplicative? or break
         for ls in range(5):
             a_t = a - alpha*d; clip 0,1000; normalize to sum n.
             ft = fast_max(a_t) # compute conv
             if ft < M0 - 1e-12 or ft <= M0 - 1e-10:
                 accept; M0=ft; break
             alpha *=0.5
         if no accept:
             # try opposite? maybe use smoothing or perturb.
       # periodically exact evaluate a if fast_score < best_exact? Use exact to update best; also if many iterations.
      return best_a, score
  # Run optimize from top initializations with time slices. Maybe 1st parent 600s, 2nd best sinusoid 200s, random 100s. If parent not available, use ones/sinusoids.
  # Return best exact sequence list clipped.

Direct gradient function:
```
def grad_top(a, idx, w, n):
    C = np.empty(n)
    C.fill(0) # maybe reuse? allocate each iter
    # for each k, val in zip(idx,w):
    for k, wk in zip(idx, w):
        k=int(k)
        if wk ==0: continue
        jmin = max(0, k - n + 1)
        jmax = min(n - 1, k)
        if jmin > jmax: continue
        # assign C[jmin:jmax+1] += wk * a[k - jmax : k - jmin + 1 : -1]
        start = k - jmax
        stop = k - jmin - 1  # exclusive for negative step?
        # In Python a[start:stop:-1] gives indices start, start-1,... > stop. Need stop < start.
        # if start == k-jmax, end index k-jmin. We want include k-jmin.
        # For negative step, slice stop is one before end: k-jmin -1.
        seg = a[start : (k - jmin - 1) : -1]
        C[jmin:jmax+1] += wk * seg
    return C
```

We can implement a robust active-set subgradient:
- Keep list `active_idx` of lag indices to penalize, and weights maybe adaptive (increase for higher c).
- Start with top K peaks at initial.
- Each iter compute B. Identify any new peak above `M_current - tol` and add. Or keep top K highest among B∪active.
- Compute gradient C from active set with weights decreasing for below peaks.
- Line search on max.
This focuses on worst lags encountered, prevents ignoring emerging peaks.

A simple effective algorithm:
```
active = top 128 peaks
for iter:
  B = conv(a)
  M = B.max()
  # Update active to top K peaks by B (maybe K=128) with weights exp((B-M)/T)
  # Could blend with previous active: keep if still high
  C = grad_from_active
  d = C - mean(C)
  line search add/mult
```
Top K each iter already includes all current peaks; no need persistent. But if a peak was active earlier and now below top K, no longer penalized; okay if not max.

Line search score via FFT. Need `fast_B(a)` returns B length L. Reuse? We'll implement:
```
def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
N_conv = next_pow2(L)
def conv_fft(a):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N_conv)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N_conv)
    return c[:L]
```
Rounding small imaginary? `irfft` returns real.

`score_from_B(B) = 2.0 * n * B.max() / S^2` but if normalized S=n, S^2=n^2, score=2*M/n. For arbitrary sum after normalization, use `2*n*M/(S*S)`. We'll maintain S=n; after renormalization exactly n (within floating). Use direct for exact.

Exact score on array:
```
def exact_score_array(a):
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<0.01: return inf
    B=np.convolve(a,a)
    return 2*n*B.max()/(S*S)
```
This matches evaluator except no Python list and no per element min? We clip. For final list, `max(0,min(1000,x))`. Good.

Line search details with multiplicative:
```
def try_step(a, d, M_cur, B, w, active_idx, max_ls=6):
    # d = C - weighted_mean perhaps raw
    scale = np.max(np.abs(d))
    if scale <=1e-15: return None
    d = d / scale
    alphas = [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.025,0.0125,0.00625]
    best_candidate=None; best_val=inf
    for alpha in alphas:
        factor = np.exp(-alpha*d)
        factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-12, 1e12)
        a_new = a * factor
        a_new = np.clip(a_new, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = a_new.sum()
        if S <= 1e-12: continue
        a_new *= n/S
        B_new = conv_fft(a_new)
        M_new = B_new.max()
        G_new = np.dot(B_new, w) # w length L sparse? If w full? Use active weights sum.
        # acceptable if M_new <= M_cur + 1e-10 and G_new < G_cur - 1e-12? But G_cur maybe current weighted sum.
        if M_new < M_cur - 1e-9:
            return a_new, B_new, M_new, alpha
        # store best by (M_new, G_new)
        val = (M_new, G_new)
        if val < (best_val[0], best_val[1]): ...
    if best_candidate and M_new <= M_cur*(1+1e-6) and G_new < G_cur*(1-1e-12): accept best
    else return None
```

Let's design schedule in detail for optimizer:
```
def optimize(a0, max_time, initial_K=64, max_K=512, name=''):
    a = normalize(a0)
    B = conv_fft(a); M = B.max(); S=n
    best_a = a.copy(); best_M = M; last_exact = 0; best_exact_from_external?
    # We'll pass global best_exact to update? Use nonlocal.
    iter=0; stall=0
    K = initial_K
    active_idx = []; w=[]
    # If time < 5 return.
    while time < stop:
       # adapt K: if stall>50: increase K or reset
       if iter % 200 ==0: maybe cycle K = [32,64,128,256,512]
       # compute active weights from B
       idx, w = get_top_weights(B, K_ratio=...)
       C = grad_active(a, idx, w)
       # gradient direction: weighted mean projection
       S = n (since normalized)
       # For multiplicative direction, maybe use d = C - weighted_mean
       wm = np.dot(a, C)/S
       d = C - wm
       # Maybe add small L2 regularization toward parent/best to avoid drift? d += λ*(a - a_center)? Not.
       # line search multiplicative
       accepted=False
       for alpha in alphas...
       if accepted: M_new, B_new; if M_new < best_M - 1e-10: best_a=...; stall=0; exact check
             if M_new < M - tol: M=M_new, B=B_new, a=a_new
             stall +=? no step if no M improve
       else:
          stall +=1
          if stall > 10:
             # perturb current: add noise; recompute B/M
          if stall > 100:
             # reset to best_a and change K
    return best_a
```

def active_weights(B, K):
    L=len(B); M=B.max()
    if K >= L: idx all
    else: idx = np.argpartition(-B, K-1)[:K]
    w = np.zeros(L)
    Bt = B[idx]
    # sort desc for temp
    Bt_sorted = np.sort(Bt)[::-1]
    if Bt_sorted.size>0:
       # temp = max(Bt_sorted[0] - Bt_sorted[-1], M*1e-4, 1e-6)
       # But if top K not all near max? Bt_sorted[-1] could far below M if sharp peaks; then weights concentrate on highest few. Good.
       temp = max(Bt_sorted[0] - Bt_sorted[-1], M*1e-4)
       # cap exponent: exp((Bt-M)/temp)
       e = (Bt - M) / temp
       e = np.maximum(e, -50)
       vals = np.exp(e)
       vals /= vals.sum()
       w[idx] = vals
    # symmetrize
    w = 0.5*(w + w[::-1])
    w *= 2.0 / (w.sum())? Wait sym of sum1 gives sum1. Yes.
    return w, w>0
```

Direct gradient from w_arr nonzero:
```
def grad_from_weights(a, w, n, idx):
    C = np.zeros(n)
    idx = np.flatnonzero(w)
    for k in idx:
       wk = w[k]
       if wk ==0: continue
       k=int(k)
       jmin = k - n + 1
       if jmin <0: jmin=0
       jmax = k
       if jmax > n-1: jmax = n-1
       if jmin > jmax: continue
       start = k - jmax
       end = k - jmin
       C[jmin:jmax+1] += wk * a[start:end+1][::-1]
    return C
```
For each k, if wk small and segment length large, cost. Could skip wk<1e-8? Since weights normalized over <=1024, min maybe underflow; skip small.
Optimization of slicing: `a[start:end+1][::-1]` creates view? Negative step returns view? It may create copy? It's a view with stride -1. Then `wk * view` creates temp. We do `C[sl] += wk * seg`. Good.
Could avoid temp by `C[sl] *=1?` no. Fine.

Line search with multiplicative and full weights:
```
def line_search(M, B, w, a):
   C=grad...
   wm = np.dot(a,C)/n  # if sum n exactly
   d=C-wm
   # If d too small, return None
   scale=np.max(np.abs(d))
   if scale < 1e-12: return None
   d=d/scale
   Gcur = np.dot(B,w)
   best = None
   for alpha in [0.3,0.15,0.075,0.0375,0.01875,0.009,0.0045]:
       f = np.exp(-alpha*d)
       f = np.clip(f, 1e-12, 1e12)
       an = a*f
       an = np.clip(an, 0,1000)
       sn = an.sum()
       if sn < 1e-12: continue
       an *= n/sn
       Bn = conv_fft(an)
       Mn = Bn.max()
       Gn = np.dot(Bn,w)
       # Accept if Mn < M - tol_abs
       if Mn < M - 1e-9:
           return an,Bn,Mn, alpha
       # If Gn significantly lower and Mn not worse too much
       if best is None or (Mn,Gn)<(best[2],best[3]): best=(an,Bn,Mn,Gn)
   if best and best[2] <= M*(1+1e-6) and best[3] < Gcur*(1-1e-10): return best
   return None
```
But `np.dot(Bn,w)` full 14k each alpha, okay. Since w symmetric and nonnegative. We can use active idx to get G faster: `Gn = Bn[idx].dot(w[idx])`; but full okay. Dot 14k ~0.00005s.

```
def grad_fft(a,w,Ng):
   w_rev = w[::-1]
   A = np.fft.rfft(a, Ng)
   W = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, Ng)
   h = np.fft.irfft(A*W, Ng)
   return h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```

    import numpy as np, time, math
    start=time.monotonic(); hard_deadline=start+max(1,budget_s)-10
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # get parent
    try: n = len(height_sequence_1); parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    except: n=6962; parent=np.ones(n)
    if n<2: n=64? fallback? Use 6962? If parent invalid, n=6962.

    # if parent length okay; else n=parent.size

Let's implement `make_weights(B, top_pct)`:
```
def make_weights(B, top_pct):
    M = B.max()
    L=len(B)
    if top_pct <= 0 or top_pct >=1:
       return np.ones(L)/L? But top_pct 0.5 gives T= M - median maybe.
    r = int(L*(1-top_pct))
    if r <0: r=0
    if r >= L: thr=B.min()
    else:
       # Use partition to avoid sort
       thr = np.partition(B, r)[-1]
    T = M - thr
    min_T = 1e-8*M + 1e-12
    if T < min_T: T = min_T
    # Also cap T to avoid all-equal weights if M-thr tiny: if T small, exp all ~1. That's okay; but then active all. For top_pct small and sharp peak, T small; good.
    x = (B - M) / T
    # To avoid underflow and all zeros if M<thr? 
    x = np.clip(x, -700, 0) # But values B-M <=0; if B has round >M due to max? M max, no. Sym? no.
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = w.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0:
       w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(B))]=1
    else: w/=s
    return w, T
```
If top_pct=0.05, thr=95th percentile. Coefficients above thr (top 5%) have exponent between 0 and -T/T=-1? Actually those at thr get -1, max 0, so top 5% weights from exp(-1) to1; below < -1 decays. This is good.

import numpy as np, time, math

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s)) - 10.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # parent
    parent = None
    try:
       if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
           p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
           if p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
               parent=p
    except: pass
    if parent is None or parent.size==0:
       parent = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)
    parent = np.clip(parent,0.0,1000.0)
    n = parent.size
    if n<10: n=6962; parent=np.ones(n)
    L=2*n-1
    Nconv = 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    Ngrad = 1 << (n+L-2).bit_length()
    # helpers
```

Optimization function (uses FFT score but global exact best):
```
def optimize(a0, stop):
   a = normalize(a0.copy())
   B = conv_fft(a); M = B.max()
   a_best = a.copy(); M_best = M
   top_pct_list = [0.05, 0.02, 0.10, 0.20, 0.01, 0.30]
   top_idx = 0
   cur_alpha=0.1
   stall=0
   last_perturb=time.monotonic()
   last_exact=time.monotonic()
   best_exact_local? We'll rely global.
   while time.monotonic() < stop:
      # if current M > best M + 2%, reset to best and small perturb
      if M > M_best * (1.02) + 1e-8:
          a = a_best.copy() + rng.normal(0, 0.01, n); a=normalize(a); B=conv_fft(a); M=B.max()
          stall=5
      # make weights
      top_pct = top_pct_list[top_idx % len]
      w, T = make_weights(B, top_pct)
      Gcur = float(np.dot(B,w))
      C = grad_fft(a,w)
      wm = float(np.dot(a,C)/n)
      d = C - wm
      scale = np.max(np.abs(d))
      improved_this_iter=False
      if scale > 1e-12:
         d /= scale
         # alphas adaptive
         alphas = [cur_alpha, cur_alpha*0.5, cur_alpha*0.25, cur_alpha*0.125, cur_alpha*0.0625]
         # if cur_alpha too small, include 0.001
         alphas = [al for al in alphas if al > 1e-5]
         best_candidate=None; best_key=None
         for al in alphas:
             # if time left
             factor = np.exp(-al*d)
             an = a * factor
             an = np.clip(an, 0.0, 1000.0)
             an = np.maximum(an, 1e-8)
             s = an.sum()
             if s < 1e-12: continue
             an *= n/s
             Bn = conv_fft(an)
             Mn = float(Bn.max())
             Gn = float(np.dot(Bn,w))
             accept = False
             if Mn < M - 1e-8*M:
                 accept=True
             elif Mn <= M + 1e-6*M and Gn < Gcur - 1e-10*max(1.0,Gcur):
                 accept=True
             if accept:
                 # update
                 a=an; B=Bn; M=Mn; cur_alpha=min(0.5, al*1.2) if accept due to M? else cur_alpha
                 if Mn < M_best:
                     M_best=Mn; a_best=a.copy(); stall=0
                 else:
                     stall+=1
                 improved_this_iter=True
                 break
         if not improved_this_iter:
             # no accept
             cur_alpha = max(0.001, cur_alpha*0.5)
             stall += 1
             # try different top_pct every few stalls
             if stall % 5 == 0:
                 top_idx += 1
             if stall > 15 and time - last_perturb > 2.0:
                 sigma = 0.01 + 0.01*min(stall,10)
                 a = a + rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
                 a = normalize(a)
                 B = conv_fft(a); M = B.max()
                 if M < M_best:
                    M_best=M; a_best=a.copy()
                 last_perturb=time.monotonic()
                 stall = max(1, stall-10)
             if stall > 80:
                 # reset to best and switch top
                 a = a_best.copy()
                 B = conv_fft(a); M = B.max()
                 top_idx += 1
                 cur_alpha=0.1
                 stall=10
      else:
         # gradient tiny, perturb
         ...
      # exact check local best every 5s or if M_best improved
      if time.monotonic() - last_exact > 5.0:
          # exact eval a_best maybe expensive; update nonlocal best_seq? We can't modify outer easily but use list.
```

Let's detail `make_weights`:
```
def make_weights(B, top_pct):
    M = float(B.max())
    L = B.size
    if top_pct < 0.01:
        # one? But we'll use 0.01 min
    r = int(L * (1.0 - top_pct))
    if r < 0: r = 0
    if r >= L: r = L-1
    # Use partition. To handle ties and avoid O(L log),
    thr = float(np.partition(B, r)[-1])
    T = M - thr
    epsT = 1e-8 * max(1.0, M)
    if T < epsT:
        T = epsT
    # Also cap T so top_pct actually not too broad? If M-thr huge? e.g., top_pct 0.5, thr median, T maybe. No cap needed.
    x = (B - M) / T
    # due to round, x maybe >0, clip
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       w = np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(B))] = 1.0
    else:
       w /= s
    return w, T, M
```
If top_pct=0.4, r=60%. Top 40 above threshold; weights from 1 to e^-1. Below decays. Good.
We might want to ensure sum not dominated by single due to exp clip; fine.

Top_pct list: Let's include `0.05, 0.02, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.01, 0.5`. Starting 0.05.

```
def attempt_update(a,B,M,w,Gcur,cur_alpha):
   C = grad_fft(a,w)
   wm = dot(a,C)/n
   d = C - wm
   scale = max(abs(d))
   if scale <= 1e-12: return None, cur_alpha, 'flat'
   dn = d / scale
   # choose alphas based on cur_alpha
   alphas = []
   a0 = max(1e-4, min(0.5, cur_alpha))
   # Need maybe start small if last no accept. Use [a0, a0*0.5, ...]
   for mult in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125]:
       al = a0*mult
       if al > 1e-5: alphas.append(al)
   factor cache? no.
   best = None
   for al in alphas:
       f = np.exp(-al * dn)
       # Instead of clip f to [1e-12,1e12], with al <=0.5, dn [-1,1], safe.
       an = a * f
       s = an.sum()
       if s <= 0: continue
       an = an * (n/s)
       an = np.clip(an, 0, 1000)
       an = np.maximum(an, 1e-9)
       s=an.sum(); an*=n/s
       Bn = conv_fft(an)
       Mn = float(Bn.max())
       Gn = float(np.dot(Bn, w))
       # decision
       accept = False
       reason = None
       if Mn < M - 1e-8*max(1,M):
          accept=True; reason='M'
       elif Mn <= M + 1e-6*max(1,M) and Gn < Gcur - 1e-10*max(1,Gcur):
          accept=True; reason='G'
       if accept:
          return (an,Bn,Mn,al,reason), cur_alpha, None
       # store best by tuple (Mn,Gn)
       key = (Mn, Gn)
       if best is None or key < best[3]: best=(an,Bn,Mn,key)
   if best is not None and best[2] <= M*(1+1e-5) and best[2] < M -? no, and best Gn < Gcur - ...:
      maybe accept as candidate if M_new <= M+(1e-5*M)
   return None, cur_alpha, 'noaccept'
```

Lowpass random function for exploration:
```
def random_direction(n, rng, nfreq=10):
   d=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,nfreq+1):
       amp = rng.normal(scale=1.0/(k+1))
       ph = rng.uniform(0,2π)
       d += amp*(np.cos(2π*k*np.arange(n)/n + ph))
   return d
```
But use sine/cos random. Or FFT: create spectrum with amplitudes random and lowpass, irfft.
```
def smooth_noise(rng,n,nfreq=20):
   f=np.zeros(n//2+1, complex)
   ks=np.arange(n//2+1)
   mask=ks<=nfreq
   f[mask] = (rng.normal(size=nfreq+1)+1j*rng.normal(nfreq+1))
   f[0]=0
   return np.fft.irfft(f,n)
```
Normalize direction by std.

Now, code for optimizer needs access to `conv_fft`, `grad_fft`, `make_weights`, `normalize`, rng. We define nested.

Pseudo code for `optimize`:
```
def optimize(a0, stop, name=''):
    a = normalize(a0)
    B = conv_fft(a)
    M = float(B.max())
    a_best = a.copy(); M_best = M
    top_pcts = (0.05,0.02,0.10,0.20,0.35,0.01,0.50,0.08)
    ti = 0
    cur_alpha=0.1
    stall=0
    last_perturb=0.0
    # maybe track best exact? no
    while time.monotonic() < stop:
       if M > M_best*(1.005) + 1e-8:
           # reset with tiny noise
           a = a_best.copy()
           if rng.random() < 0.5:
               a = a + rng.normal(0,0.005,n)
           a = normalize(a)
           B = conv_fft(a); M=float(B.max())
           ti = (ti+1)%len(top_pcts)
           cur_alpha=0.1
           stall=5
       top_pct = top_pcts[ti % len]
       w,T,Mw = make_weights(B, top_pct)
       Gcur = float(np.dot(B,w))
       C = grad_fft(a,w)
       wm = float(np.dot(a,C)/n)
       d = C - wm
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-12:
           # flat, perturb
           a = a + rng.normal(0,0.02,n); a=normalize(a); B=conv_fft(a); M=B.max(); stall+=1; continue
       dn = d/scale
       a0_try = cur_alpha
       alphas = [a0_try, a0_try*0.5, ...]
       accepted=False
       for al in alphas:
           if time.monotonic() > stop-2: break
           f = np.exp(-al*dn)
           an = a*f
           an = np.maximum(an, 1e-9)
           s=an.sum()
           if s<=0: continue
           an *= n/s
           an = np.clip(an,0,1000)
           s=an.sum(); if s<=0 continue
           an *= n/s
           Bn=conv_fft(an)
           Mn=float(Bn.max())
           Gn=float(np.dot(Bn,w))
           if Mn < M - 1e-8*max(1.0,M) or (Mn < M*(1+1e-5) and Gn < Gcur*(1-1e-9)):
               a=an; B=Bn; M=Mn; 
               if Mn < M_best:
                   M_best=Mn; a_best=a.copy(); stall=0
               else:
                   stall += 1
               cur_alpha = min(0.5, al*1.5)
               accepted=True
               break
       if not accepted:
           cur_alpha = max(1e-4, a0_try*0.5)
           stall += 1
           if stall % 7 == 0:
               ti += 1
           if stall > 20:
               # perturb small
               sigma = 0.01 + 0.003*min(stall,20)
               a = a + rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
               a=normalize(a)
               B=conv_fft(a); M=B.max()
               if M < M_best: M_best=M; a_best=a.copy(); stall=0
               else: stall = max(0, stall-5)
           if stall > 100:
               a = a_best.copy()
               B = conv_fft(a); M=B.max()
               ti = (ti+1)%len; cur_alpha=0.1; stall=10
    return a_best.copy(), M_best
```
Need be careful: if accepted surrogate with M increased, `stall+=1`; if many surrogate small increases, stall triggers perturb. Good.

Maybe if accepted M decrease but not best? impossible if M_cur was best? If current M could be above best due to surrogate, then M decrease to current M maybe still >best; stall increments. That's okay; eventually reset if >best+0.5%.

Polish function:
```
def polish_direct(a0, stop):
   a=normalize(a0); B=direct_conv(a); M=B.max(); a_best=a.copy(); M_best=M
   top_pcts=(0.05,0.02,0.10,0.20)
   ti=0; cur_alpha=0.05; stall=0
   while time<stop:
      if M > M_best*(1+0.002)+1e-9: reset best
      w,_=make_weights(B, top_pcts[ti])  # uses np.partition
      Gcur=dot(B,w)
      C=grad_fft(a,w)
      d=C-dot(a,C)/n; scale...
      if scale<...: perturb
      else:
         accepted=False
         for al in [cur_alpha, cur_alpha/2, cur_alpha/4]:
            an=...; Bn=direct_conv(an); Mn=Bn.max(); Gn=dot(Bn,w)
            if Mn < M - 1e-11*max(1,M): update accepted
         if not accepted:
             ti=(ti+1)%len; stall+=1; if stall>10 perturb
      if time<stop-2: continue else break
   return a_best.copy()
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s)) - 20.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent_raw = None
    try:
        h = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
        if h is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
            if arr.size > 0:
                parent_raw = arr
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None
    if parent_raw is None:
        parent_raw = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)
    parent_raw = np.where(np.isfinite(parent_raw), parent_raw, 0.0)
    parent_raw = np.clip(parent_raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(parent_raw.size)
    if n < 2:
        n = 6962
        parent_raw = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2*n - 1
    Nconv = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    Ngrad = 1 << (3*n - 3).bit_length()
    ...
```

```
    def normalize(a):
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = np.resize(arr, n) # maybe
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            arr = arr * (float(n) / s)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-8)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s <= 1e-12:
                arr = np.ones(n)
            else:
                arr *= float(n)/s
        return arr
```

`smooth_noise` for random:
```
    def smooth_noise(low=12, scale=1.0):
        f = np.zeros(n//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
        m = min(low, n//2)
        if m > 0:
            f[1:m+1] = (rng.normal(size=m) + 1j*rng.normal(size=m)) * 0.5
        x = np.fft.irfft(f, n)
        if np.std(x) > 1e-12:
            x *= scale / np.std(x)
        return x
```
Use in random candidates and perturb.

`conv_fft`:
```
    def conv_fft(a):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, Nconv)
        B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, Nconv)
        B = B[:L]
        # remove tiny negative
        # np.maximum(B, 0) can allocate; use B[B<0]=0 if any
        if B.size:
            np.maximum(B, 0.0, out=B)
        return B
```
`np.maximum` out=B modifies. Good.

`grad_fft`:
```
    def grad_fft(a,w):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, Ngrad)
        W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], Ngrad)
        h = np.fft.irfft(A * W, Ngrad)
        return h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Returning view? Negative stride view of h; okay. Could `.copy()`? Not necessary, but to avoid h deallocated? Slicing view references h; h local remains referenced by view base? Yes, view keeps base. But we only use C immediately. Fine.

`make_weights`:
```
    def make_weights(B, top_pct, M=None):
        if M is None: M=float(B.max())
        Lb=B.size
        if top_pct <= 0: r=Lb-1
        else:
           r = int(Lb * (1.0 - top_pct))
           if r <0: r=0
           elif r >= Lb: r=Lb-1
        thr = float(np.partition(B, r)[-1])
        T = M - thr
        epsT = 1e-8 * max(1.0, M)
        if T < epsT: T = epsT
        x = (B - M) / T
        # x should <=0; clip
        np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0, out=x)
        w = np.exp(x)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            w = np.zeros(Lb, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(B))] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= s
        return w, T
```
`np.clip` with out=x requires x float array; yes.

`direct_conv`:
```
    def direct_conv(a):
        return np.convolve(a, a)
```

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        t = idx / float(n - 1)
        # also u = idx / n for periodic
    u = idx / float(n)
    for freq in (1,2,3,4):
       for phase in (0.0, np.pi/2, np.pi):
          for amp in (0.5, 1.0):
             cands.append(1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*u + phase))
             cands.append(1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*u + phase))
    for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0):

       cands.append(1.0 + amp*np.sin(np.pi*t))

Pseudo detailed:
```
       while time.monotonic() < stop:
           if M > M_best * (1.005) + 1e-8:
               a = a_best.copy()
               if rng.random() < 0.5:
                   a = a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.005, n)
               a = normalize(a)
               B = conv_func(a); M=float(B.max())
               ti = (ti+1) % len(top_pcts)
               cur_alpha = 0.1
               stall = 5
           top_pct = top_pcts[ti % len(top_pcts)]
           w, _ = make_weights(B, top_pct, M)
           Gcur = float(np.dot(B, w))
           C = grad_fft(a, w)
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
               C = np.zeros(n)
           wm = float(np.dot(a, C) / float(n))
           d = C - wm
           d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
           scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
           if scale < 1e-12:
               a = a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
               a=normalize(a); B=conv_func(a); M=float(B.max())
               if M < M_best: M_best=M; a_best=a.copy(); stall=0
               else: stall += 3
               continue
           dn = d / scale
           a_try = cur_alpha
           # alphas
           alphas = [a_try, a_try*0.5, a_try*0.25]
           if stall > 10:
               alphas = [0.5, 0.25, a_try, a_try*0.5, 0.05, 0.02]
           elif stall > 3:
               alphas = [a_try, a_try*0.5, a_try*0.25, a_try*0.125]
           alphas = [al for al in alphas if al > 1e-5]
           if not alphas: alphas=[0.01]
           accepted = False
           for al in alphas:
               if time.monotonic() > stop - 1.0:
                   break
               f = np.exp(-al * dn)
               an = a * f
               an = np.maximum(an, 1e-9)
               s = float(an.sum())
               if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
                   continue
               an *= float(n) / s
               an = np.clip(an, 0.0, 1000.0)
               s = float(an.sum())
               if s <= 1e-12:
                   continue
               an *= float(n) / s
               Bn = conv_func(an)
               Mn = float(Bn.max())
               Gn = float(np.dot(Bn, w))
               accept = False
               if Mn < M - 1e-8 * max(1.0, M):
                   accept = True
               elif Mn < M * (1.0 + 1e-5) and Gn < Gcur * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                   accept = True
               if accept:
                   a = an; B = Bn; M = Mn
                   if Mn < M_best:
                       M_best = Mn; a_best = a.copy(); stall = 0
                   else:
                       stall += 1
                   cur_alpha = min(0.5, al * 1.5)
                   accepted = True
                   break
           if not accepted:
               cur_alpha = max(1e-4, a_try * 0.5)
               stall += 1
               if stall % 7 == 0:
                   ti = (ti + 1) % len(top_pcts)
               if use_direct:
                   # direct polish less perturb?
                   if stall > 8:
                      sigma=0.02 + 0.002*min(stall,10)
                      a = a + rng.normal(0, sigma, n)
                      a=normalize(a); B=conv_func(a); M=float(B.max())
                      if M < M_best: M_best=M; a_best=a.copy(); stall=0
                      else: stall=max(1, stall-3)
                   if stall > 40:
                      a = a_best.copy(); B=conv_func(a); M=float(B.max()); ti=(ti+1)%len; cur_alpha=0.05; stall=5
               else:
                   if stall > 20:
                       sigma = 0.01 + 0.003 * min(stall, 30)
                       a = a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                       a = normalize(a)
                       B = conv_func(a); M=float(B.max())
                       if M < M_best:
                           M_best = M; a_best = a.copy(); stall=0
                       else:
                           stall = max(1, stall - 5)

                       a = a_best.copy()
                       B = conv_func(a); M=float(B.max())
                       ti = (ti+1) % len(top_pcts)
                       cur_alpha = 0.1
                       stall = 10
       return a_best.copy(), M_best

- `f = np.zeros(n//2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)`: For `irfft`, last bin can be complex? For even n? `irfft` expects half-spectrum; last bin for even? If n even, length n//2+1 includes Nyquist which should be real for real signal, but can pass complex? `irfft` takes complex input and assumes last bin Nyquist? It may ignore imaginary?    if n % 2 == 0:
       f[-1] = 0.0
```

Let's write `prefer exact evaluator`:
```
    def exact_eval_array(a):
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = np.resize(arr, n)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        try:
            seq = arr.tolist()
            val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
            return float(val)
        except Exception:
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s < 0.01:
                return float('inf')
            B = np.convolve(arr, arr)
            return float(2.0 * n * float(B.max()) / (s * s))
```
The fallback uses visible formula. But if `evaluate_sequence` exists but is slow and returns inf due to list of np.float? list from tolist Python floats. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 12.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Parent construction, if supplied.  We only reference it, never embed it.
    parent_raw = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
            if arr.size > 0:
                parent_raw = arr
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None

    if parent_raw is None:
        parent_raw = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)

    parent_raw = np.where(np.isfinite(parent_raw), parent_raw, 0.0)
    parent_raw = np.clip(parent_raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(parent_raw.size)
    if n < 2:
        n = 6962
        parent_raw = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    Nconv = 1 << max(1, (L - 1).bit_length())
    Ngrad = 1 << max(1, (3 * n - 3).bit_length())

    def normalize(a):
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = np.resize(arr, n)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = arr * (float(n) / s)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-8)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = arr * (float(n) / s)
        if arr.size and arr.max() > 1000.0:
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s <= 1e-12:
                return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = arr * (float(n) / s)
            if arr.size and arr.max() > 1000.0:
                arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return arr

    def conv_fft(a):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, Nconv)
        B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, Nconv)
        B = B[:L]
        np.maximum(B, 0.0, out=B)
        return B

    def direct_conv(a):
        return np.convolve(a, a)

    def grad_fft(a, w):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, Ngrad)
        W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], Ngrad)
        h = np.fft.irfft(A * W, Ngrad)
        return h[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

    def make_weights(B, top_pct, M=None):
        if M is None:
            M = float(B.max())
        Lb = B.size
        if top_pct <= 0.0:
            r = Lb - 1
        else:
            r = int(Lb * (1.0 - top_pct))
            if r < 0:
                r = 0
            elif r >= Lb:
                r = Lb - 1
        thr = float(np.partition(B, r)[-1])
        T = M - thr
        epsT = 1e-8 * max(1.0, M)
        if T < epsT:
            T = epsT
        x = (B - M) / T
        np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0, out=x)
        w = np.exp(x)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            w = np.zeros(Lb, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(B))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = w / s
        return w, T

    def exact_eval_array(a):
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = np.resize(arr, n)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
            return float(val)
        except Exception:
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s < 0.01:
                return float("inf")
            B = np.convolve(arr, arr)
            return float(2.0 * n * float(B.max()) / (s * s))

    def smooth_noise(low=12, scale=1.0):
        f = np.zeros(n // 2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
        maxk = n // 2
        if maxk > 1:
            m = min(low, maxk)
            f[1:m + 1] = 0.5 * (rng.normal(size=m) + 1j * rng.normal(size=m))
            if n % 2 == 0:
                f[-1] = 0.0
        x = np.fft.irfft(f, n)
        sx = float(np.std(x))
        if sx > 1e-12:
            x = x * (scale / sx)
        return x

    # Candidate pool: parent, reversed parent, smooth parametric shapes, and
    # a few low-frequency randomized shapes.
    candidates = []
    candidates.append(parent_raw.copy())
    candidates.append(parent_raw[::-1].copy())
    candidates.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if n > 1:
        u = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)

        for freq in (1, 2, 3, 4):
            for phase in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi):
                for amp in (0.5, 1.0):
                    candidates.append(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * u + phase))
                    candidates.append(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * u + phase))

        for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
            candidates.append(1.0 + amp * np.cos(np.pi * t))
            candidates.append(1.0 + amp * np.sin(np.pi * t))

        for _ in range(6):
            candidates.append(1.0 + smooth_noise(low=16, scale=0.8))

    # Quick FFT prescreen, then exact evaluation of the best few.
    prescreen = []
    for raw in candidates:
        a = normalize(raw)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            continue
        B = conv_fft(a)
        M = float(B.max())
        prescreen.append((2.0 * M / float(n), a))

    exact_cands = []

    def add_exact(arr):
        if exact_cands and len(exact_cands) > 30:
            return
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return
        sc = exact_eval_array(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            exact_cands.append((sc, arr))

    add_exact(parent_raw)
    add_exact(normalize(parent_raw))
    add_exact(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if prescreen:
        scores = [z[0] for z in prescreen]
        order = np.argsort(scores)[:min(12, len(prescreen))]
        for i in order:
            _, a = prescreen[int(i)]
            add_exact(a)

    if not exact_cands:
        ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        exact_cands.append((exact_eval_array(ones), ones))

    exact_cands.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    best_seq = exact_cands[0][1].copy()
    best_score = float(exact_cands[0][0])

    def consider_array(arr):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        if arr is None:
            return
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return
        # Original and a tiny-zero trimmed version; both are valid and either
        # can be slightly better due to the evaluator's exact summation.
        variants = [arr]
        z = np.where(arr < 1e-7, 0.0, arr)
        if z.sum() > 0.01:
            variants.append(z)
        for a in variants:
            sc = exact_eval_array(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_seq = a.copy()

    def optimize(a0, stop, use_direct=False):
        stop = min(float(stop), hard_deadline - 15.0)
        a = normalize(a0)
        conv_func = direct_conv if use_direct else conv_fft
        B = conv_func(a)
        M = float(B.max())

        a_best = a.copy()
        M_best = M

        top_pcts = (0.05, 0.02, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.01, 0.50, 0.08)
        ti = 0
        cur_alpha = 0.08 if not use_direct else 0.04
        stall = 0

        while time.monotonic() < stop:
            if M > M_best * (1.005) + 1e-8:
                a = a_best.copy()
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    a = a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.005, n)
                a = normalize(a)
                B = conv_func(a)
                M = float(B.max())
                ti = (ti + 1) % len(top_pcts)
                cur_alpha = 0.08 if not use_direct else 0.04
                stall = 5

            top_pct = top_pcts[ti % len(top_pcts)]
            w, _ = make_weights(B, top_pct, M)
            Gcur = float(np.dot(B, w))

            C = grad_fft(a, w)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            wm = float(np.dot(a, C) / float(n))
            d = C - wm
            d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))

            if scale < 1e-12:
                a = a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
                a = normalize(a)
                B = conv_func(a)
                M = float(B.max())
                if M < M_best:
                    M_best = M
                    a_best = a.copy()
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    stall += 3
                continue

            dn = d / scale
            a_try = cur_alpha
            if stall > 10:
                alphas = [0.5, 0.25, a_try, a_try * 0.5, 0.05, 0.02]
            elif stall > 3:
                alphas = [a_try, a_try * 0.5, a_try * 0.25, a_try * 0.125]
            else:
                alphas = [a_try, a_try * 0.5, a_try * 0.25]
            alphas = [al for al in alphas if al > 1e-5]
            if not alphas:
                alphas = [0.01]

            M_old = M
            accepted = False
            for al in alphas:
                if time.monotonic() > stop - 1.0:
                    break
                f = np.exp(-al * dn)
                an = a * f
                an = np.maximum(an, 1e-9)
                s = float(an.sum())
                if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                an = an * (float(n) / s)
                an = np.clip(an, 0.0, 1000.0)
                s = float(an.sum())
                if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                an = an * (float(n) / s)

                Bn = conv_func(an)
                Mn = float(Bn.max())
                Gn = float(np.dot(Bn, w))

                accept = False
                if Mn < M - 1e-8 * max(1.0, M):
                    accept = True
                elif Mn < M * (1.0 + 1e-5) and Gn < Gcur * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                    accept = True

                if accept:
                    a = an
                    B = Bn
                    M = Mn
                    if Mn < M_best:
                        M_best = Mn
                        a_best = a.copy()
                        stall = 0
                    else:
                        stall += 1
                    if Mn < M_old:
                        cur_alpha = min(0.5, al * 1.4)
                    else:
                        cur_alpha = min(0.5, al)
                    accepted = True
                    break

            if not accepted:
                cur_alpha = max(1e-4, a_try * 0.5)
                stall += 1
                if stall % 7 == 0:
                    ti = (ti + 1) % len(top_pcts)

                if use_direct:
                    if stall > 8:
                        sigma = 0.02 + 0.002 * min(stall, 10)
                        a = a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                        a = normalize(a)
                        B = conv_func(a)
                        M = float(B.max())
                        if M < M_best:
                            M_best = M
                            a_best = a.copy()
                            stall = 0
                        else:
                            stall = max(1, stall - 3)
                    if stall > 40:
                        a = a_best.copy()
                        B = conv_func(a)
                        M = float(B.max())
                        ti = (ti + 1) % len(top_pcts)
                        cur_alpha = 0.04
                        stall = 5
                else:
                    if stall > 20:
                        sigma = 0.01 + 0.003 * min(stall, 30)
                        a = a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                        a = normalize(a)
                        B = conv_func(a)
                        M = float(B.max())
                        if M < M_best:
                            M_best = M
                            a_best = a.copy()
                            stall = 0
                        else:
                            stall = max(1, stall - 5)
                    if stall > 120:
                        a = a_best.copy()
                        B = conv_func(a)
                        M = float(B.max())
                        ti = (ti + 1) % len(top_pcts)
                        cur_alpha = 0.08
                        stall = 10

        return a_best.copy(), M_best

    # Phase 1: exploit the best initialized candidate.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = hard_deadline - now - 45.0
    if rem > 30.0:
        stop = now + 0.55 * rem
        aopt, _ = optimize(best_seq, stop, use_direct=False)
        consider_array(aopt)

    # Phase 2: try the next strongest two-level/random initialization.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = hard_deadline - now - 45.0
    if rem > 60.0 and len(exact_cands) > 1:
        second = exact_cands[1][1]
        if exact_score_gap_ok := (exact_cands[1][0] < 2.0 + 1e-9):
            stop = now + 0.25 * rem
            aopt, _ = optimize(second, stop, use_direct=False)
            consider_array(aopt)

    # Phase 3: smooth random restart from the current best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = hard_deadline - now - 45.0
    if rem > 50.0:
        noise_small = smooth_noise(low=20, scale=0.25)
        aexp = normalize(best_seq + noise_small)
        stop = now + min(180.0, 0.35 * rem)
        aopt, _ = optimize(aexp, stop, use_direct=False)
        consider_array(aopt)

    # Phase 4: a second, stronger smooth restart if time remains.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = hard_deadline - now - 45.0
    if rem > 50.0:
        noise_med = smooth_noise(low=30, scale=0.45)
        aexp = normalize(best_seq + noise_med)
        stop = now + min(120.0, 0.35 * rem)
        aopt, _ = optimize(aexp, stop, use_direct=False)
        consider_array(aopt)

    # Phase 5: exact-convolution polish.  This is slower per iterate but uses
    # the same direct convolution model as the evaluator for final refinement.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if n <= 12000 and hard_deadline - now > 70.0:
        stop = hard_deadline - 25.0
        aopt, _ = optimize(best_seq, stop, use_direct=True)
        consider_array(aopt)

    # Final acceptance set: best, parents, ones, and zero-trimmed variants.
    consider_array(best_seq)
    consider_array(parent_raw)
    consider_array(normalize(parent_raw))
    consider_array(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    final_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    final_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(final_arr), final_arr, 0.0)
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final_arr.size == 0 or float(final_arr.sum()) < 0.01:
        final_arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    out = [float(x) for x in final_arr]

    try:
        sc = evaluate_sequence(out)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            out = [1.0] * n
            sc = evaluate_sequence(out)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                out = [1.0]
    except Exception:
        out = [1.0] * max(1, n)

    return out
```
